Three-way neutral pairing: NACA RM A52E01 fig. 8 (a) and (b), readings A (round 15-A), B (round 15-B), C (round 17)
Named by 0014 section 10.7 entry (ac), second part. Readings committed in 1182556 (A, B) and 8a55ee4 (C).
Rule: two values agree when |X - Y| <= e_X + e_Y, each reader's stated error (2 sigma or bound) as written. Same
method as the two-way pairing (pairing-2026-10-26-a51j11-a52e01, cccd251), applied to the pairs A-B, A-C and B-C.
Curve identity from the values; drawn curves are the curves joined by value links. Nothing decided by majority,
nothing averaged, enveloped or resolved toward any reading. Items only one reader gives are READ ONCE.

Format of pairs_<figure>.csv: the columns of cccd251's pairs files come first, unchanged in name, order and
meaning (verdict, diff_A_minus_B and sum_of_errors are the A-B comparison exactly as before; also_agrees_with now
names, for each reader's row, the curves of the other readings outside this drawn curve that agree with it, as
'A row agrees with B ...'). label_check now gives the three readers' wing calls for the drawn curve. New columns are
appended after B_note: drawn_curve, readers (which readers have a row), C's row (C_series ... C_flag), the A-C and
B-C comparisons (verdict_A_C, diff_A_minus_C, sum_of_errors_A_C, and the alpha terms for ends; the same for B-C)
C_note, and last summary (on alpha 0 rows: the drawn curve's alpha 0 status). Rows are one per drawn curve and station
(or end side, or alpha 0) per match group: at one station the
A and B rows are paired nearest first as before, then C's rows are attached nearest first to those groups;
leftover rows stand alone. category is station, end or alpha 0 as before. identity_map.csv is now one row per
drawn curve with the three readers' curves, links, wing calls and alpha 0 values (its columns are new; it replaces
the A -> B partner rows of cccd251's identity_map.csv for this figure).

Links (new for three readings): two curves are joined when both directions map them to each other, or one maps
them and the other direction maps that curve to no curve; a contested one-way map is not a link and is listed.
Curves the station rule cannot link get a supplementary check (common stations, ends, alpha 0) against their best
candidate, labelled as the pairer's check and not used for drawn curves or pairs. 'station vs end' items (a row
compared with the other reader's end, the cccd251 rule) are listed apart from station pairs.

A-B against cccd251: the A-B comparisons are those of cccd251, except in 8(a) M = 1.2, 1.4, 1.7, where A's 'M=1.4
line c' and B's 'M = 1.4; Wing 2' (a tie in cccd251, so not paired there) are now one drawn curve through C's
'a14-K' and are compared (8 of 10 stations agree, 1 end pair); the counts below include them.

C's file as written: six box rows of 8(a) M=0.9 (a09-Ld-001, -003, -004 and a09-Rr-002, -004, -005, alpha 5.5 to
6.0, the X crossing) give x = -1.7905 or -1.7906, outside the axis (0 to -0.8). They are paired as written and
produce the station disagreements listed at alpha 5.5 and 5.75 to 6.0 in that panel; they look like a slip in C's
file, which only C can correct. No other row of any reading lies outside the axes.
